  <!-- ============================================================
       4. WHY MOST FOUNDERS TRY PR AND FAIL
       ============================================================ -->
  <section class="emos-section emos-section--bordered">
    <div class="emos-container">
      <div class="emos-section-header emos-section-header--center emos-fade-in">
        <span class="emos-label">The Real Problem</span>
        <h2 class="emos-title">Why most founders try PR and fail.</h2>
        <p class="emos-subtitle">Four failure modes show up over and over, no matter the industry. EMOS is built to remove all four.</p>
      </div>

      <div class="emos-fail-grid">
        <div class="emos-fail emos-fade-in">
          <div class="emos-fail__title">1. Generic mass-pitching with no founder POV</div>
          <div class="emos-fail__body">Sending the same press release to 200 journalists. No angle, no expertise, no reason for anyone to respond. Modern editors filter this in under five seconds.</div>
        </div>
        <div class="emos-fail emos-fade-in">
          <div class="emos-fail__title">2. A $4K&ndash;$10K/month agency that takes the knowledge with them</div>
          <div class="emos-fail__body">Pay a retainer, get monthly reports, never see what&#8217;s actually being sent on your behalf. When the contract ends, every relationship, template, and tracking sheet walks out the door.</div>
        </div>
        <div class="emos-fail emos-fade-in">
          <div class="emos-fail__title">3. Journalist outreach platforms used sporadically</div>
          <div class="emos-fail__body">Most founders try HARO, Qwoted, or Featured casually. No system, no schedule, no review of what worked. After 20 unanswered queries, the tab gets closed. The platform was fine; the consistency wasn&#8217;t.</div>
        </div>
        <div class="emos-fail emos-fade-in">
          <div class="emos-fail__title">4. Cold-emailing journalists with no framework</div>
          <div class="emos-fail__body">Treating reporters like prospects. Pitching the company instead of the story. Getting ignored or blocked, then concluding &#8220;press doesn&#8217;t work for us.&#8221; Press worked. The pitch didn&#8217;t.</div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <!-- ============================================================

        <h2 class="emos-title">How investors read your media presence.</h2>
        <p class="emos-subtitle">Press coverage is not vanity. To a VC running a five-minute pre-meeting search, what they find is already a signal, whether you intended it to be or not.</p>

        <p class="emos-subtitle">Most founders treat a press placement as a one-off win. EMOS extracts five compounding returns from every single one. Which is why one Forbes citation can keep paying for years.</p>
      </div>

      <div class="emos-returns-grid emos-stagger">

        </div>
        <div class="emos-return">
          <div class="emos-return__num">2</div>
          <div class="emos-return__title">LLM Citation</div>
          <div class="emos-return__body">ChatGPT, Perplexity, and Google AI Overviews cite credible publications. Your name surfaces in AI answers about your space.</div>
        </div>
        <div class="emos-return">
          <div class="emos-return__num">3</div>
          <div class="emos-return__title">Sales Collateral</div>

        </div>
        <div class="emos-return">
          <div class="emos-return__num">4</div>
          <div class="emos-return__title">Investor Proof</div>
          <div class="emos-return__body">Quoted credibility for the Series A round. VCs read what&#8217;s in the press; founders cited in Tier 1 publications skip the &#8220;who is this?&#8221; question.</div>
        </div>
        <div class="emos-return">
          <div class="emos-return__num">5</div>
          <div class="emos-return__title">Social Signal</div>
          <div class="emos-return__body">Share-worthy content for LinkedIn and X. Earned coverage compounds organic reach and personal-brand momentum.</div>
        </div>

  <section class="emos-section emos-section--bordered" id="faq" style="background: var(--emos-bg-elevated);">
    <div class="emos-container">
      <div class="emos-section-header emos-section-header--center emos-fade-in">
        <span class="emos-label">FAQ</span>
        <h2 class="emos-title">Common questions</h2>
      </div>
      <div class="emos-faq-list emos-fade-in">

        <div class="emos-faq-item">
          <button class="emos-faq-question" onclick="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('is-open')">How much time does this require each week?</button>
          <div class="emos-faq-answer"><strong>Plan for 3 to 5 hours per week:</strong> 90 minutes for the live session plus 2 to 3 hours for assignments. The assignments aren&#8217;t busywork. They&#8217;re real pitches going to real journalists. Most founders pair the system with a part-time VA ($300 to $1,500/month) who handles execution while they own the strategy.</div>
        </div>

        <div class="emos-faq-item">
          <button class="emos-faq-question" onclick="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('is-open')">I don&#8217;t have a team member who can run this. What do I do?</button>
          <div class="emos-faq-answer"><strong>Accelerate includes a VA sourcing and training module.</strong> We help you identify what support you need, source the right person, and train them on the system. The real qualification isn&#8217;t whether you have someone today. It&#8217;s whether you&#8217;re willing to hire one after the program ends.</div>
        </div>

        <div class="emos-faq-item">
          <button class="emos-faq-question" onclick="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('is-open')">I tried PR before and it didn&#8217;t work. Why is this different?</button>
          <div class="emos-faq-answer"><strong>Most failed PR efforts share four traits:</strong> generic mass pitching, no founder POV, no consistent system, and total dependency on an agency that takes the knowledge with them. EMOS is built specifically to remove all four. You&#8217;ll write pitches for journalists&#8217; readers (not your own ego), run a tracked weekly cadence, and own the system permanently, with or without a VA.</div>
        </div>

        <div class="emos-faq-item">
          <button class="emos-faq-question" onclick="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('is-open')">Is this really better than just hiring a PR agency?</button>
          <div class="emos-faq-answer"><strong>Different thing.</strong> An agency runs pitches on your behalf for $24K to $60K a year indefinitely, and the knowledge leaves when the relationship does. EMOS is a one-time investment that builds the capability inside your company. Some clients do both (EMOS for in-house, our Booster service for specific campaigns). Most don&#8217;t need to.</div>
        </div>

        <div class="emos-faq-item">
          <button class="emos-faq-question" onclick="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('is-open')">Will this work for my industry?</button>
          <div class="emos-faq-answer"><strong>Yes, provided you have customers, results, or a defensible point of view.</strong> The system has produced placements for SaaS, fintech, healthcare, marketplaces, e-commerce, AI, mobility, education, and consumer products. The mechanics are the same; what changes is the publication list and the angles you&#8217;ll lead with. During the program, we map both for your specific industry.</div>
        </div>

        <div class="emos-faq-item">
          <button class="emos-faq-question" onclick="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('is-open')">When can I expect my first placement?</button>
          <div class="emos-faq-answer"><strong>Most participants submit their first pitches in Week 2 and land their first verified placement within 4 to 6 weeks of cohort end.</strong> Tier 1 placements (TechCrunch, Forbes, HBR) generally take 60 to 120 days from a cold start. The guarantee is built around these realities, not around marketing claims.</div>
        </div>

        <div class="emos-faq-item">
          <button class="emos-faq-question" onclick="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('is-open')">How exactly does the placement guarantee work?</button>
          <div class="emos-faq-answer"><strong>Complete all sessions, submit the required number of pitches, and if you don&#8217;t hit the required placements within the stated timeframe, we refund the full investment.</strong> Foundation: 15 pitches, 1 placement in 60 days. Accelerate: 30 pitches, 2 placements in 90 days. Proof of effort is your tracking spreadsheet. In 12+ years, this system has never failed to produce placements for anyone who worked it consistently.</div>
        </div>

        <div class="emos-faq-item">
          <button class="emos-faq-question" onclick="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('is-open')">Will this still work as AI changes search?</button>
          <div class="emos-faq-answer"><strong>Earned media is the most resilient channel against AI-driven shifts.</strong> LLMs (ChatGPT, Perplexity, Google AI Overviews) are trained on credible publications. When your name appears in Forbes, HBR, or Business Insider, AI systems cite you as a source. Same EEAT signals Google uses to decide who ranks. Paid ads and SEO blog content don&#8217;t carry the same weight in LLM training data. Earned editorial coverage does. EMOS doesn&#8217;t just survive the AI shift; it benefits from it.</div>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>

<script data-cfasync="false" src="/cdn-cgi/scripts/5c5dd728/cloudflare-static/email-decode.min.js"></script><script>
(function() {
  'use strict';

  function initCanvas(id) {
    var c = document.getElementById(id);
    if (!c) return;
    var x = c.getContext('2d');
    var w, h, pts;
    var MAX = 35, CONN = 120, SPD = 0.25;
    var paused = false;

    function resize() {
      w = c.width = c.offsetWidth;
      h = c.height = c.offsetHeight;
      pts = [];
      for (var i = 0; i < MAX; i++) {
        pts.push({
          x: Math.random() * w,
          y: Math.random() * h,
          vx: (Math.random() - 0.5) * SPD,
          vy: (Math.random() - 0.5) * SPD
        });
      }
    }

    function draw() {
      if (paused) return;
      x.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
      for (var i = 0; i < pts.length; i++) {
        var p = pts[i];
        p.x += p.vx;
        p.y += p.vy;
        if (p.x < 0 || p.x > w) p.vx *= -1;
        if (p.y < 0 || p.y > h) p.vy *= -1;
        x.beginPath();
        x.arc(p.x, p.y, 1.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        x.fillStyle = 'rgba(26, 122, 173, 0.15)';
        x.fill();
        for (var j = i + 1; j < pts.length; j++) {
          var q = pts[j];
          var d = Math.hypot(p.x - q.x, p.y - q.y);
          if (d < CONN) {
            x.beginPath();
            x.moveTo(p.x, p.y);
            x.lineTo(q.x, q.y);
            x.strokeStyle = 'rgba(26, 122, 173,' + (0.08 * (1 - d / CONN)) + ')';
            x.lineWidth = 0.6;
            x.stroke();
          }
        }
      }
      requestAnimationFrame(draw);
    }

    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function() {
      paused = document.hidden;
      if (!paused) requestAnimationFrame(draw);
    });
    window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
    resize();
    requestAnimationFrame(draw);
  }
  initCanvas('hero-canvas');

  var counterObs = new IntersectionObserver(function(entries) {
    entries.forEach(function(e) {
      if (e.isIntersecting) {
        var el = e.target;
        var target = parseInt(el.getAttribute('data-target'));
        if (isNaN(target)) return;
        var suffix = el.getAttribute('data-suffix') || '';
        var format = el.getAttribute('data-format') || '';
        var start = 0;
        var dur = 1800;
        var steps = 60;
        var step = Math.ceil(target / steps);
        var t = setInterval(function() {
          start += step;
          if (start >= target) { start = target; clearInterval(t); }
          var display;
          if (format === 'abbr' && start >= 1000000) display = (start / 1000000).toFixed(1) + 'M';
          else if (format === 'abbr' && start >= 1000) display = Math.round(start / 1000) + 'K';
          else display = start.toLocaleString();
          el.textContent = display + suffix;
        }, dur / steps);
        counterObs.unobserve(el);
      }
    });
  }, { threshold: 0.5 });
  document.querySelectorAll('.emos-stat-strip__value[data-target]').forEach(function(el) { counterObs.observe(el); });
  document.querySelectorAll('.emos-case-study-card__big-stat[data-target]').forEach(function(el) { counterObs.observe(el); });

  var stickyCta = document.getElementById('sticky-cta');
  if (stickyCta) {
    window.addEventListener('scroll', function() {
      if (window.scrollY > 600) stickyCta.classList.add('is-visible');
      else stickyCta.classList.remove('is-visible');
    });
  }

  var fadeObs = new IntersectionObserver(function(entries) {
    entries.forEach(function(entry) {
      if (entry.isIntersecting) entry.target.classList.add('is-visible');
    });
  }, { threshold: 0.1, rootMargin: '0px 0px -40px 0px' });
  document.querySelectorAll('.emos-fade-in, .emos-stagger').forEach(function(el) { fadeObs.observe(el); });

  var backToTop = document.querySelector('.emos-back-to-top');
  if (backToTop) {
    window.addEventListener('scroll', function() {
      if (window.scrollY > 600) backToTop.classList.add('is-visible');
      else backToTop.classList.remove('is-visible');
    });
  }

  document.querySelectorAll('.emos-page a[href^="#"]').forEach(function(link) {
    link.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
      var targetId = this.getAttribute('href');
      if (targetId === '#') return;
      var target = document.querySelector(targetId);
      if (target) {
        e.preventDefault();
        target.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
        var navLinks = document.querySelector('.emos-nav__links');
        if (navLinks) navLinks.classList.remove('is-open');
      }
    });
  });
})();
</script>
<script>
(function(){
  var ann = document.querySelector('.emos-announcement');
  var nav = document.querySelector('.emos-nav');
  var spacer = document.querySelector('.emos-nav-spacer');
  if (!nav || !spacer) return;
  function setH(){
    var annH = ann ? ann.offsetHeight : 0;
    nav.style.top = annH + 'px';
    spacer.style.height = (annH + nav.offsetHeight) + 'px';
  }
  setH();
  window.addEventListener('resize', setH);
})();
</script>
